# 2.1 Minimal MatCore Metadata

<p class="mc-version" data-version="0.5.0">Version 0.5.0</p>

The minimal MatCore metadata includes aspects of a dataset deemed important to report for data findability and reuse and are *universal* across all computational methods. The list of minimal metadata is tabulated below.

<span class="mc-legend"><span class="mc-key"><span class="mc-star">*</span> required</span><span class="mc-key"><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span> appears at most once</span><span class="mc-key"><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span> repeatable</span></span>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.creator" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">creator<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

A primary entity (e.g. person, organization, software agent) responsible for creating the dataset's core content and their institutional affiliation if relevant.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| yes | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.creator.name" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">name<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The name of the entity that generated the dataset.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Examples: `Albert Einstein`, `Lawrence Livermore National Laboratory`, `Claude Opus 5.5`.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.creator.identifier" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">identifier</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

A unique, persistent identifier that globally and unambiguously identifies the entity that created the dataset.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.creator.identifier.scheme" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">scheme<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The identifier scheme that the value belongs to.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `ORCID`, `ROR`, `DOI`, `SWHID`, `ISNI`. Other values are allowed. For individuals, an ORCID ID; for organizations, an ROR ID; for software or AI agents, a Digital Object Identifier (DOI) generated specifically for the software code or version (e.g., a Zenodo software DOI) or a Software Heritage ID (SWHID) pointing to the exact software release or state. An ISNI may identify either individuals or organizations.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.creation-date" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">creation-date<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The calendar date and time when the dataset was created.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Pattern: `\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01])(T([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d:[0-5]\dZ)?`. Examples: `2021-02-22`, `2021-02-22T14:30:00Z`. A date (YYYY-MM-DD), or a date and time in UTC (YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ssZ), following the [ISO 8601 Date and Time Format Standard](https://www.iso.org/iso-8601-date-and-time-format.html).

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.formula" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">formula<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The chemical formula of the material component.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Pattern: `[A-Za-z0-9.(){}+-]+`. Examples: `Si`, `SiO2`, `H2O2`, `Si0.7Ge0.3`, `Si{x}Ge{1-x}`, `(H2O)0.9(C2H6O)0.1`, `Ja0.8Jb0.2`. A formula is a sequence of terms. A term is an element symbol, a user-defined species or a group in parentheses, each followed by an optional count. An element symbol is one of the 118 symbols of the IUPAC Periodic Table of the Elements (listed with atomic numbers 1 to 118 in the [Standard Atomic Weights](https://www.ciaaw.org/atomic-weights.htm) table of the IUPAC Commission on Isotopic Abundances and Atomic Weights). A user-defined species, for particles that are not chemical elements (e.g. in a Lennard-Jones model), is `J` followed by lowercase letters (e.g. `Ja`, `Jlj`); no element symbol begins with J. A count is a positive number, whole or decimal (e.g. `H2O`, `Si0.7Ge0.3`), or an expression in braces (e.g. `Si{x}Ge{1-x}`). A count of 1 is omitted. An expression contains at least one variable, given in `variable`, and is linear in its variables; it is built from numbers, one-letter variables, `+`, `-`, implied multiplication and parentheses (e.g. `1-x`, `3x`, `2(1-x)`). A variable is used only when the composition varies, so a fixed composition is written with its numbers (e.g. `Si0.3Ge0.7`). A formula that is a sequence of two or more groups is a mixture of substances: the groups' counts are their mole fractions and sum to 1 (e.g. `(H2O)0.9(C2H6O)0.1`). Elements may appear in any order. A molecule is written with its molecular formula (e.g. `H2O2`, not `HO`). Separate phases, e.g. a film on a substrate, are separate material components.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.topology" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">topology</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The structure of a substance in the material component, given by an identifier, when its formula alone does not determine it.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

Note: Give topology only when the formula matches more than one isomer or polymorph and the data must say which, e.g. ethanol and dimethyl ether (C2H6O), or calcite and aragonite (CaCO3).

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.topology.substance" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">substance<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The formula of the substance, either a group in the material component's formula or the whole formula.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Examples: `C2H6O`.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.topology.identifier" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">identifier<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

An identifier of the substance's structure.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| yes | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.topology.identifier.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The identifier, in the form Annex I gives for its scheme when the scheme is listed there.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Examples: `InChI=1S/C2H6O/c1-2-3/h3H,2H2,1H3`, `CCO`, `ABC3_oP20_62_c_c_cd`.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.topology.identifier.scheme" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">scheme<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The identifier scheme that the value belongs to.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `AFLOW`, `InChI`, `SMILES`. Other values are allowed. For a molecule, InChI is preferred where it can represent it; SMILES otherwise or in addition. For a crystal, the AFLOW prototype label.

</details>

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.form" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">form<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The geometric form of the component as modeled.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `2D material`, `Bulk`, `Chain`, `Cluster`, `Molecule`, `Monolayer`, `Nanoparticle`, `Nanoribbon`, `Nanotube`, `Nanowire`, `Slab`, `Thin film`. Other values are allowed. One of: "2D material" – A crystalline solid that is a single unit cell thick (typically a few nanometers or less), while extending infinitely or to large dimensions in the lateral plane, e.g. graphene. "Bulk" – A material extending without bound in all three directions, e.g. a periodic crystal or a liquid. "Chain" – A one-dimensional sequence of atoms or monomers, e.g. an isolated polymer chain or a monatomic chain. "Cluster" – A finite aggregate of atoms or molecules, smaller than a nanoparticle. "Molecule" – A single molecule. "Monolayer" – A single layer of atoms or molecules deposited on another material. "Nanoparticle" – A solid particle of nanometer size in all three directions. "Nanoribbon" – A strip of a 2D material of nanometer width. "Nanotube" – A hollow cylinder of nanometer diameter, e.g. a carbon nanotube. "Nanowire" – A solid wire of nanometer diameter, extending without bound along its axis. "Slab" – A truncated bulk structure that can be used, for example, to model surfaces. "Thin film" – A layer of finite thickness, free-standing or on a substrate. The form is that of the component as modeled, e.g. a substrate modeled as a slab is a Slab.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.role" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">role</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The part the component plays in the structure.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Adsorbate`, `Core`, `Film`, `Inclusion`, `Layer`, `Matrix`, `Shell`, `Solute`, `Solvent`, `Substrate`. Other values are allowed. One of: "Adsorbate" – A molecule, cluster or particle on the surface of a substrate. "Core" – The inner part of a particle, enclosed by a shell. "Film" – A layer of finite thickness on a substrate. "Inclusion" – A material enclosed in a matrix, e.g. a precipitate or an embedded nanotube. "Layer" – One of the layers of a layered structure, e.g. a heterostructure, a superlattice or a stack of 2D materials. "Matrix" – A material that encloses one or more inclusions. "Shell" – A layer enclosing a core. "Solute" – A material dissolved in a solvent. "Solvent" – A liquid in which a solute is dissolved. "Substrate" – A material on which a film, layer or adsorbate lies. Give a role for each component when the structure has more than one.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.fraction" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">fraction</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The atomic fraction of the structure made up of this component, i.e. the number of atoms in the component divided by the number of atoms in the structure.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.fraction.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The value of the property.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | float | [] | dimensionless | — |

From 0 to 1. The fractions of a structure's components sum to 1, for the entries without a qualifier and for each of Initial, Final and Average.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.fraction.qualifier" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">qualifier</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

What the value represents.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Average`, `Final`, `Initial`, `Maximum`, `Minimum`. Other values are allowed. Without a qualifier, the value holds throughout the simulation and across the system, and the entry is the only one for the condition. Otherwise give one entry for each qualifier that applies, e.g. Initial and Final for a ramp, Minimum and Maximum for a range. For a vector or tensor, Minimum and Maximum are taken component by component.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.phase" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">phase</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

A phase of the material.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | yes | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Amorphous`, `Single crystal`, `Polycrystal`, `Quasicrystal`, `Liquid`, `Gas`, `Plasma`. Other values are allowed. One of: "Amorphous" – A solid material without crystalline order. "Single crystal" – A solid material characterized by a repeating pattern of atoms that extends throughout it without interruption. "Polycrystal" – A solid material made of more than one crystalline grain of differing orientation. "Quasicrystal" – A solid material with long-range order but without translational periodicity, e.g. one whose diffraction pattern shows five-fold rotational symmetry. "Liquid" – A fluid structure (e.g. the molten state of a material). "Gas" – A state where molecules or atoms are far apart and only interact during collisions. "Plasma" – A gas comprised of ions and electrons. Omit for a component whose form is Molecule.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.description" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">description</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

An explanation of the nature of the material, e.g. a crystal structure designation, a chemical formula for a molecule, etc.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

e.g. a surface facet such as Pt(111), an orientation relationship such as (111)Pt ∥ (001)CeO₂, or the chirality of a nanotube.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.structure.material-component.microstructure" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">microstructure</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

For a solid state phase, a description of the arrangement of phases, grains and defects in the material.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

</details>

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">computation<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

A description of a computer simulation used to generate data in the dataset.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| yes | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.method-class" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">method-class<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The general category to which the computational technique belongs.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Electronic`, `Atomistic`, `Mesoscopic`, `Continuum`, `Data-driven`. Other values are allowed.

Note: The categories listed above are based on the [MODA standard](https://data.europa.eu/doi/10.2777/404734).

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.method" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">method<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The computational materials science (CMS) approach used in the computation.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Depends on `method-class`. For `Electronic`: Recommended: `CC`, `QMC`, `DFT`, `MBPT`. Other values are allowed. For `Atomistic`: Recommended: `MC`, `MD`. Other values are allowed. For `Mesoscopic`: Recommended: `DDD`, `KMC`, `CGMD`. Other values are allowed. For `Continuum`: Recommended: `PF`. Other values are allowed. For `Data-driven`: Recommended: `ML`. Other values are allowed. "CC" – Coupled Cluster; "QMC" – Quantum Monte Carlo; "DFT" – Density Functional Theory; "MBPT" – Many-Body Perturbation Theory methods (such as GW and BSE); "MC" – Monte Carlo; "MD" – Molecular Dynamics; "DDD" – Discrete Dislocation Dynamics; "KMC" – Kinetic Monte Carlo; "CGMD" – Coarse-grained MD; "PF" – Phase Field; "ML" – Machine Learning.

Note: Give the method that produces the configurations or properties of the dataset. When another method supplies its energies and forces, give that one in `interaction-model`.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.interaction-model" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">interaction-model</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The model that supplies the energies and forces used by `method`, when it is a different method.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | yes | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `CC`, `DFT`, `Empirical potential`, `ML potential`, `QMC`, `Tight binding`. Other values are allowed. "CC" – Coupled Cluster; "DFT" – Density Functional Theory; "Empirical potential" – An interatomic potential with a fixed functional form fitted to data, e.g. Lennard-Jones or EAM; "ML potential" – A machine-learned interatomic potential, e.g. GAP or MACE; "QMC" – Quantum Monte Carlo; "Tight binding" – A semi-empirical electronic-structure model, e.g. DFTB. Give one entry per model, e.g. DFT and Empirical potential for a QM/MM simulation.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">simulation-conditions<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The conditions imposed on the simulated system during the computation, e.g. by a thermostat, a barostat, a loading device or a fixed simulation cell.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| yes | no |

Note: The conditions below, other than type and description, are given as appropriate to the simulation. Each is imposed on the simulation as an external constraint. A quantity computed from the simulation, e.g. a heat flux measured under an imposed temperature gradient or the temperature of a microcanonical run, is a result, not a condition, and is not given here. A condition that varies is given as several entries, each with a qualifier; the description explains how it varies.

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.type" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">type<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The nature of the simulation being performed.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| yes | no |

Note: Detailed explanations of the simulation conditions are given in `description`.

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.type.thermodynamic-state" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">thermodynamic-state<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

Whether the simulated system is at equilibrium, in a steady state, or evolving in time.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Equilibrium`, `Steady state`, `Transient`. Other values are allowed. One of: "Equilibrium" – The system samples thermodynamic equilibrium. "Steady state" – A nonequilibrium state whose averages do not change in time, maintained by a driving such as a strain rate or a heat flux. "Transient" – A state whose averages change in time, e.g. relaxation toward equilibrium or a steady state, or a quench.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.type.system-openness" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">system-openness</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

What the system exchanges with its surroundings.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Isolated`, `Closed`, `Open`. Other values are allowed. One of: "Isolated" – Exchanges neither energy nor particles, e.g. the microcanonical ensemble. "Closed" – Exchanges energy but not particles, e.g. the canonical or isothermal-isobaric ensemble. "Open" – Exchanges particles, or their species, with a reservoir, e.g. the grand-canonical or semi-grand-canonical ensemble.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.type.time-dependence" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">time-dependence</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

How the imposed conditions change during the simulation.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Constant`, `Varying`, `Cyclic`. Other values are allowed. One of: "Constant" – No imposed condition changes. "Varying" – An imposed condition changes, e.g. a temperature ramp or a change of ensemble. "Cyclic" – An imposed condition changes periodically, e.g. cyclic loading.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.type.spatial-uniformity" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">spatial-uniformity</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

How the imposed conditions vary across the system.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Uniform`, `Nonuniform`, `Periodic`. Other values are allowed. One of: "Uniform" – Every imposed condition is the same throughout the system. "Nonuniform" – An imposed condition differs between parts of the system, e.g. a thermostat applied to part of it. "Periodic" – An imposed condition varies periodically across the system, e.g. sinusoidal heating. Not to be confused with `cell-periodicity`.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.description" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">description</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

Explanatory text clarifying the simulation type, and how any condition varies in space or time, e.g. a temperature ramp, a thermostat applied to part of the system, or a composition set by chemical potentials. For a condition that varies, it states how, e.g. the shape of a ramp or the region a value applies to.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.number-of-particles" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">number-of-particles</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The number of particles (atoms or other discrete entities) in the simulated system, as imposed by the simulation.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.number-of-particles.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The value of the property.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | int | [] | dimensionless | — |

The number of particles <i>N</i>.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.number-of-particles.qualifier" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">qualifier</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

What the value represents.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Average`, `Final`, `Initial`, `Maximum`, `Minimum`. Other values are allowed. Without a qualifier, the value holds throughout the simulation and across the system, and the entry is the only one for the condition. Otherwise give one entry for each qualifier that applies, e.g. Initial and Final for a ramp, Minimum and Maximum for a range. For a vector or tensor, Minimum and Maximum are taken component by component.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.volume" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">volume</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The volume of the simulated system, as imposed by the simulation.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.volume.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The value of the property, in the unit given by `unit`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | float | [] | Volume | angstrom^3 |

The volume <i>V</i>.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.volume.unit" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">unit<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The unit of the value, an Annex U symbol whose Kind is the Kind of `value`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.volume.qualifier" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">qualifier</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

What the value represents.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Average`, `Final`, `Initial`, `Maximum`, `Minimum`. Other values are allowed. Without a qualifier, the value holds throughout the simulation and across the system, and the entry is the only one for the condition. Otherwise give one entry for each qualifier that applies, e.g. Initial and Final for a ramp, Minimum and Maximum for a range. For a vector or tensor, Minimum and Maximum are taken component by component.

</details>

</details>

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.cell" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">cell</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The simulation cell, as imposed by the simulation.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.cell.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The value of the property, in the unit given by `unit`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | float | [3,3] | Length | angstrom |

Ordered list of three vectors (a, b, c), each with three components, that define the simulation cell. `value[i][j]` is the j-th Cartesian component, in the frame of the simulation, of the i-th cell vector, so the rows are a, b and c. The vectors (a, b, c) need not be orthogonal, and the magnitudes (|a|, |b|, |c|) are the cell vector lengths. The unit applies to all nine components.

</details>

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Average`, `Final`, `Initial`, `Maximum`, `Minimum`. Other values are allowed. Otherwise give one entry for each qualifier that applies, e.g. Initial and Final for a ramp, Minimum and Maximum for a range. <details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.cell-reference" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">cell-reference</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The simulation cell in the reference configuration, relative to which strain and strain rate are defined.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.cell-reference.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The value of the property, in the unit given by `unit`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | float | [3,3] | Length | angstrom |

Ordered list of three vectors (a<sub>0</sub>, b<sub>0</sub>, c<sub>0</sub>), each with three components, that define the cell used in the simulation in its reference configuration. `value[i][j]` is the j-th Cartesian component, in the frame of the simulation, of the i-th reference cell vector, so the rows are a<sub>0</sub>, b<sub>0</sub> and c<sub>0</sub>. The vectors (a<sub>0</sub>, b<sub>0</sub>, c<sub>0</sub>) need not be orthogonal, and the magnitudes (|a<sub>0</sub>|, |b<sub>0</sub>|, |c<sub>0</sub>|) are the reference cell vector lengths. The unit applies to all nine components.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.cell-periodicity" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">cell-periodicity</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

Whether periodic boundary conditions are applied along each cell vector, for both cell and cell-reference.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | bool | [3] | inapplicable | — |

Ordered list of three booleans (<i>p</i><sub>a</sub>, <i>p</i><sub>b</sub>, <i>p</i><sub>c</sub>), which are set to true if periodic boundary conditions are applied along the corresponding cell vectors (a, b, c).

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.temperature.unit" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">unit<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The unit of the value, an Annex U symbol whose Kind is the Kind of `value`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.temperature.qualifier" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">qualifier</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

What the value represents.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Average`, `Final`, `Initial`, `Maximum`, `Minimum`. Other values are allowed. Without a qualifier, the value holds throughout the simulation and across the system, and the entry is the only one for the condition. Otherwise give one entry for each qualifier that applies, e.g. Initial and Final for a ramp, Minimum and Maximum for a range. For a vector or tensor, Minimum and Maximum are taken component by component.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.stress" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">stress</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The stress, normal and shear components, imposed on the simulated system, e.g. by a barostat or an external loading device.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

Note 1: Requires cell, which defines the basis of the tensor components.

Note 2: The adopted sign convention is for tensile normal stresses to be positive, and compressive normal stresses to be negative. This implies that for a material subjected to a positive pressure <i>p</i>, the stress tensor components are [-<i>p</i>, -<i>p</i>, -<i>p</i>, 0, 0, 0].

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.stress.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The value of the property, in the unit given by `unit`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | float | [6] | Pressure | GPa |

The components of the (symmetric) Cauchy stress tensor <i>σ</i> in the following order: [<i>σ</i><sub>11</sub>, <i>σ</i><sub>22</sub>, <i>σ</i><sub>33</sub>, <i>σ</i><sub>23</sub>, <i>σ</i><sub>13</sub>, <i>σ</i><sub>12</sub>]. The stress components are expressed relative to an orthonormal basis (e<sub>1</sub>, e<sub>2</sub>, e<sub>3</sub>), where e<sub>1</sub> is in the direction of a, e<sub>2</sub> is in the direction of c╳a, and e<sub>3</sub> = e<sub>1</sub>╳e<sub>2</sub>, where (a, b, c) are the vectors defining the cell, which may not be orthogonal. The unit applies to all six components.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.stress.unit" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">unit<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.stress.qualifier" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">qualifier</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

What the value represents.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Average`, `Final`, `Initial`, `Maximum`, `Minimum`. Other values are allowed. Without a qualifier, the value holds throughout the simulation and across the system, and the entry is the only one for the condition. Otherwise give one entry for each qualifier that applies, e.g. Initial and Final for a ramp, Minimum and Maximum for a range. For a vector or tensor, Minimum and Maximum are taken component by component.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.strain" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">strain</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The strain imposed on the simulation cell relative to cell-reference, as the Lagrangian strain tensor.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

Note: Requires cell-reference, which defines the basis of the tensor components.

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.strain.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The value of the property.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | float | [6] | dimensionless | — |

The components of the (symmetric) Lagrangian strain tensor <i>E</i> in the following order: [<i>E</i><sub>11</sub>, <i>E</i><sub>22</sub>, <i>E</i><sub>33</sub>, <i>E</i><sub>23</sub>, <i>E</i><sub>13</sub>, <i>E</i><sub>12</sub>]. The strain components are expressed relative to an orthonormal basis (e<sub>1</sub>, e<sub>2</sub>, e<sub>3</sub>), where e<sub>1</sub> is in the direction of a<sub>0</sub>, e<sub>2</sub> is in the direction of c<sub>0</sub>╳a<sub>0</sub>, and e<sub>3</sub> = e<sub>1</sub>╳e<sub>2</sub>, where (a<sub>0</sub>, b<sub>0</sub>, c<sub>0</sub>) are the vectors defining the cell in the reference configuration, which may not be orthogonal.

</details>

What the value represents.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Average`, `Final`, `Initial`, `Maximum`, `Minimum`. Other values are allowed. Without a qualifier, the value holds throughout the simulation and across the system, and the entry is the only one for the condition. Otherwise give one entry for each qualifier that applies, e.g. Initial and Final for a ramp, Minimum and Maximum for a range. For a vector or tensor, Minimum and Maximum are taken component by component.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.strain-rate" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">strain-rate</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

Note: Requires cell-reference, which defines the basis of the tensor components.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|

The components of the (symmetric) Lagrangian strain rate tensor <i>Ė</i> in the following order: [<i>Ė</i><sub>11</sub>, <i>Ė</i><sub>22</sub>, <i>Ė</i><sub>33</sub>, <i>Ė</i><sub>23</sub>, <i>Ė</i><sub>13</sub>, <i>Ė</i><sub>12</sub>]. The strain rate components are expressed relative to an orthonormal basis (e<sub>1</sub>, e<sub>2</sub>, e<sub>3</sub>), where e<sub>1</sub> is in the direction of a<sub>0</sub>, e<sub>2</sub> is in the direction of c<sub>0</sub>╳a<sub>0</sub>, and e<sub>3</sub> = e<sub>1</sub>╳e<sub>2</sub>, where (a<sub>0</sub>, b<sub>0</sub>, c<sub>0</sub>) are the vectors defining the cell in the reference configuration, which may not be orthogonal.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.heat-flux" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">heat-flux</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The heat flux imposed on the simulated system, the thermal energy per unit area per unit time driven through it, e.g. by exchanging energy between a heat source and a heat sink.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

Note: Give heat-flux or temperature-gradient, whichever the simulation imposes; the other is a result.

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.heat-flux.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The value of the property, in the unit given by `unit`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | float | [3] | HeatFluxDensity | W/m^2 |

The components of the heat flux vector <i>q</i>. The heat flux components are expressed relative to an orthonormal basis (e<sub>1</sub>, e<sub>2</sub>, e<sub>3</sub>), where e<sub>1</sub> is in the direction of a, e<sub>2</sub> is in the direction of c╳a, and e<sub>3</sub> = e<sub>1</sub>╳e<sub>2</sub>, where (a, b, c) are the vectors defining the cell, which may not be orthogonal.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.heat-flux.qualifier" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">qualifier</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

What the value represents.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Average`, `Final`, `Initial`, `Maximum`, `Minimum`. Other values are allowed. Without a qualifier, the value holds throughout the simulation and across the system, and the entry is the only one for the condition. Otherwise give one entry for each qualifier that applies, e.g. Initial and Final for a ramp, Minimum and Maximum for a range. For a vector or tensor, Minimum and Maximum are taken component by component.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.temperature-gradient" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">temperature-gradient</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The temperature gradient imposed on the simulated system, e.g. by thermostatting two regions at different temperatures.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

Note: Give heat-flux or temperature-gradient, whichever the simulation imposes; the other is a result.
