Note: Requires cell, which defines the basis of the vector components.

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.body-force.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The value of the property, in the unit given by `unit`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | float | [3] | Acceleration | m/s^2 |

The components of the body force per unit mass <i>g</i>. The body force components are expressed relative to an orthonormal basis (e<sub>1</sub>, e<sub>2</sub>, e<sub>3</sub>), where e<sub>1</sub> is in the direction of a, e<sub>2</sub> is in the direction of c╳a, and e<sub>3</sub> = e<sub>1</sub>╳e<sub>2</sub>, where (a, b, c) are the vectors defining the cell, which may not be orthogonal.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.body-force.unit" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">unit<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The unit of the value, an Annex U symbol whose Kind is the Kind of `value`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.body-force.qualifier" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">qualifier</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

What the value represents.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Average`, `Final`, `Initial`, `Maximum`, `Minimum`. Other values are allowed. Without a qualifier, the value holds throughout the simulation and across the system, and the entry is the only one for the condition. Otherwise give one entry for each qualifier that applies, e.g. Initial and Final for a ramp, Minimum and Maximum for a range. For a vector or tensor, Minimum and Maximum are taken component by component.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.electrode-potential" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">electrode-potential</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The potential of the working electrode imposed on the simulated system, e.g. by a potentiostat in a constant-potential simulation, relative to the reference given in `reference`.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.electrode-potential.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The value of the property, in the unit given by `unit`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | float | [] | ElectricPotential | V |

The potential of the working electrode relative to `reference`.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.electrode-potential.unit" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">unit<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The unit of the value, an Annex U symbol whose Kind is the Kind of `value`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.electrode-potential.reference" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">reference<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The reference against which the potential is given.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `SHE`, `RHE`, `Vacuum`. Other values are allowed. One of: "SHE" – The standard hydrogen electrode. "RHE" – The reversible hydrogen electrode, at the pH of the simulation. "Vacuum" – The vacuum level, giving an absolute potential.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.electrode-potential.qualifier" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">qualifier</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

What the value represents.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Average`, `Final`, `Initial`, `Maximum`, `Minimum`. Other values are allowed. Without a qualifier, the value holds throughout the simulation and across the system, and the entry is the only one for the condition. Otherwise give one entry for each qualifier that applies, e.g. Initial and Final for a ramp, Minimum and Maximum for a range. For a vector or tensor, Minimum and Maximum are taken component by component.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.chemical-potential" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">chemical-potential</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

The chemical potential of a species imposed on the simulated system by a reservoir, e.g. in a grand-canonical or semi-grand-canonical simulation.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.chemical-potential.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The value of the property, in the unit given by `unit`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | float | [] | Energy | eV |

The chemical potential <i>μ</i> per particle, or the difference <i>μ</i>(species) − <i>μ</i>(relative-to). An absolute value is relative to the energy zero of the model given in `interaction-model`, so absolute values from different models do not compare. A value in a molar unit (e.g. kJ/mol) is converted to an energy per particle before it is given.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.chemical-potential.unit" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">unit<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The unit of the value, an Annex U symbol whose Kind is the Kind of `value`.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.chemical-potential.species" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">species<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The species whose chemical potential is given, as an element symbol, a user-defined species or a group of a material component's formula.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Examples: `Cu`, `H2O`, `Ja`.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.simulation-conditions.chemical-potential.relative-to" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">relative-to</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

A second species, when the value is the difference between the chemical potentials of species and of this one.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Note: Give relative-to when the simulation imposes a difference of chemical potentials, as a semi-grand-canonical simulation does.

</details>

What the value represents.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Average`, `Final`, `Initial`, `Maximum`, `Minimum`. Other values are allowed. Without a qualifier, the value holds throughout the simulation and across the system, and the entry is the only one for the species. Otherwise give one entry for each qualifier that applies, e.g. Initial and Final for a ramp, Minimum and Maximum for a range. For a vector or tensor, Minimum and Maximum are taken component by component.

</details>

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.software" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">software<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

A program, package or script used to perform the calculations: the simulation code, a calculation method or workflow framework that drives it, or a custom script.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| yes | yes |

Note: Give one entry for each layer that took part, outermost first. A file is given under the entry that reads it; for a script, its own code is its file.

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.software.name" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">name<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The name of the software package.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.software.role" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">role</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The part this software played in the calculations.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Calculation method`, `Script`, `Simulation code`, `Workflow framework`. Other values are allowed. One of: "Calculation method" – A packaged procedure that computes a defined property, e.g. an OpenKIM test or an iprPy calculation. "Script" – Custom code written for the calculations, e.g. a Python script using ASE. "Simulation code" – A program that performs the simulation or calculation itself, e.g. LAMMPS or VASP. "Workflow framework" – A system that sets up, runs and manages calculations with other software, e.g. pyiron, AiiDA or MedeA.

</details>

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |

The version of the component.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.software.dependency.role" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">role</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The kind of component.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Compiler`, `FFT library`, `Interpreter`, `Library`, `Linear algebra library`, `MPI library`. Other values are allowed. One of: "Compiler" – Translates source code into an executable, e.g. GCC or the Intel oneAPI compilers. "FFT library" – Computes fast Fourier transforms, e.g. FFTW. "Interpreter" – Runs a script, e.g. Python. "Library" – Any other library the software calls, e.g. LibXC or ASE. "Linear algebra library" – Provides BLAS, LAPACK or ScaLAPACK routines, e.g. OpenBLAS or Intel MKL. "MPI library" – Implements the Message Passing Interface (MPI), e.g. Open MPI or MPICH.

</details>

</details>

| no | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.software.file.encoding" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">encoding</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

How contents is encoded.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Base64`. Other values are allowed.

Note: Without encoding, contents is the file's text. With Base64, contents is the file's bytes encoded as Base64 (RFC 4648); use it for a binary file, or for a text file whose exact bytes matter.

</details>

| Required | Repeatable |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.computation.software.file.checksum.algorithm" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">algorithm<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The algorithm that computed the checksum.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `SHA1`, `SHA224`, `SHA256`, `SHA384`, `SHA512`, `SHA3-256`, `SHA3-384`, `SHA3-512`, `BLAKE2b-256`, `BLAKE2b-384`, `BLAKE2b-512`, `BLAKE3`, `MD2`, `MD4`, `MD5`, `MD6`, `ADLER32`. Other values are allowed. The algorithm names of [SPDX 2.3.1 §8.4](https://spdx.github.io/spdx-spec/v2.3.1/file-information/) (File checksum field).

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.provenance" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">provenance</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

History of the dataset, detailing its origins and transformations.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

Note: It is recommended to record the provenance of the dataset and to keep it current with each change.

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.provenance.event-type" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">event-type<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

Description of change made to the dataset.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `Initial creation`, `Admin update`, `Version update`, `Metadata update`. Other values are allowed. "Initial creation" – Initial creation of the dataset. "Admin update" – Editorial update to the dataset not requiring a version update (e.g. spelling correction). "Version update" – Change to the dataset or associated files requiring a new version. "Metadata update" – Change to the metadata, but not the dataset itself.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.provenance.date" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">date<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The date and time when the change was made.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Pattern: `\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01])(T([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d:[0-5]\dZ)?`. Examples: `2021-02-22`, `2021-02-22T14:30:00Z`. A date (YYYY-MM-DD), or a date and time in UTC (YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ssZ), following the [ISO 8601 Date and Time Format Standard](https://www.iso.org/iso-8601-date-and-time-format.html).

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.provenance.agent" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">agent<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

An entity (e.g. person, organization, software agent) responsible for the change, and their institutional affiliation if relevant.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| yes | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.provenance.agent.name" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">name<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The name of the entity that made the change.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Examples: `Albert Einstein`, `Lawrence Livermore National Laboratory`, `Claude Opus 5.5`.

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.provenance.agent.identifier" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">identifier</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| no | yes |

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.provenance.agent.identifier.value" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">value<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The identifier, in the form Annex I gives for its scheme when the scheme is listed there.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.provenance.agent.identifier.scheme" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">scheme<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The identifier scheme that the value belongs to.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `ORCID`, `ROR`, `DOI`, `SWHID`, `ISNI`. Other values are allowed. For individuals, an ORCID ID; for organizations, an ROR ID; for software or AI agents, a Digital Object Identifier (DOI) generated specifically for the software code or version (e.g., a Zenodo software DOI) or a Software Heritage ID (SWHID) pointing to the exact software release or state. An ISNI may identify either individuals or organizations.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.provenance.agent.affiliation" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">affiliation</span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|

<summary><span class="mc-name">algorithm<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The algorithm that computed the checksum.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `SHA1`, `SHA224`, `SHA256`, `SHA384`, `SHA512`, `SHA3-256`, `SHA3-384`, `SHA3-512`, `BLAKE2b-256`, `BLAKE2b-384`, `BLAKE2b-512`, `BLAKE3`, `MD2`, `MD4`, `MD5`, `MD6`, `ADLER32`. Other values are allowed. The algorithm names of [SPDX 2.3.1 §8.4](https://spdx.github.io/spdx-spec/v2.3.1/file-information/) (File checksum field).

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.matcore-author.identifier.scheme" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">scheme<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-once" title="appears at most once" aria-label="appears at most once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 12c0-1-.26-1.96-.7-2.8l1.46-1.46A7.93 7.93 0 0 1 20 12c0 1.39-.36 2.68-1 3.82l-1.5-1.5c.32-.72.5-1.49.5-2.32M2.39 1.73 1.11 3 5.5 7.37C4.55 8.68 4 10.27 4 12c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12c0-1.17.34-2.26.92-3.19l8.27 8.27c-.93.58-2.02.92-3.19.92v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3c1.73 0 3.32-.55 4.63-1.5l4.21 4.23 1.27-1.27zM12 6v2.8l.1.1L16 5l-4-4v3c-1.38 0-2.68.36-3.82 1l1.5 1.5c.72-.32 1.5-.5 2.32-.5"/></svg></span></summary>

The identifier scheme that the value belongs to.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Recommended: `ORCID`, `ROR`, `DOI`, `SWHID`, `ISNI`. Other values are allowed. For individuals, an ORCID ID; for organizations, an ROR ID; for software or AI agents, a Digital Object Identifier (DOI) generated specifically for the software code or version (e.g., a Zenodo software DOI) or a Software Heritage ID (SWHID) pointing to the exact software release or state. An ISNI may identify either individuals or organizations.

</details>

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.matcore-date" markdown="1">

The calendar date and time when this MatCore document was created.

| Required | Repeatable | Type | Extent | Kind | Rec. unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| yes | no | string | [] | inapplicable | — |

Pattern: `\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01])(T([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d:[0-5]\dZ)?`. Examples: `2021-02-22`, `2021-02-22T14:30:00Z`. A date (YYYY-MM-DD), or a date and time in UTC (YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ssZ), following the [ISO 8601 Date and Time Format Standard](https://www.iso.org/iso-8601-date-and-time-format.html).

</details>

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.license" markdown="1">
<summary><span class="mc-name">license<span class="mc-star" title="required" aria-label="required">*</span></span><span class="mc-icon mc-i-rep" title="repeatable: may appear more than once" aria-label="repeatable: may appear more than once"><svg width="18" height="18" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 6v3l4-4-4-4v3a8 8 0 0 0-8 8c0 1.57.46 3.03 1.24 4.26L6.7 14.8A5.9 5.9 0 0 1 6 12a6 6 0 0 1 6-6m6.76 1.74L17.3 9.2c.44.84.7 1.8.7 2.8a6 6 0 0 1-6 6v-3l-4 4 4 4v-3a8 8 0 0 0 8-8c0-1.57-.46-3.03-1.24-4.26"/></svg></span></summary>

A contract defining the terms and conditions under which the dataset can be used.

| Required | Repeatable |
|---|---|
| yes | yes |

Note: A license expression as defined in the [SPDX specification, version 2.3.1](https://spdx.github.io/spdx-spec/v2.3.1/SPDX-license-expressions/), built from current identifiers of the [SPDX License List](https://spdx.org/licenses/): a single identifier (e.g. `CC-BY-4.0`) or a combination (e.g. `CC-BY-4.0 OR CC0-1.0`). Give the SPDX short identifier, not the license's full name, so that the value can be read by tools. A license not on the SPDX License List, including a custom license, is written `LicenseRef-` followed by letters, digits, `-` or `.` (e.g. `LicenseRef-NIST-data`), with its `name` and a `link` to its full text, both required in this case. A license for data is usually one written for data, e.g. `CC-BY-4.0`, `CC0-1.0` or `ODbL-1.0`, rather than one written for software.

<details class="note mc-property" id="minimal.license.identifier" markdown="1">

## Example: Minimal MatCore Metadata

Dataset source: [https://doi.org/10.60732/8e9bc5b0](https://doi.org/10.60732/8e9bc5b0)

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<root>
  <creator>
    <name>Albert P. Bartók</name>
    <affiliation>
      <name>Rutherford Appleton Laboratory</name>
    </affiliation>
  </creator>
  <creator>
    <name>James Kermode</name>
    <affiliation>
      <name>University of Warwick</name>
    </affiliation>
  </creator>
  <creator>
    <name>Noam Bernstein</name>
    <affiliation>
      <name>Naval Research Laboratory</name>
    </affiliation>
  </creator>
  <creator>
    <name>Gábor Csányi</name>
    <affiliation>
      <name>University of Cambridge</name>
    </affiliation>
  </creator>
  <title>Si_PRX_GAP: A foundational Density Functional Theory dataset with silicon configurations for training machine learning interatomic potentials</title>
  <creation-date>2021-02-22</creation-date>
  <description>The original DFT training data for the general-purpose silicon interatomic potential described in the associated publication. The kinds of configuration that were included were selected based on intuition and past experience to obtain good coverage pertaining to a range of properties.</description>
  <structure>
    <material-component>
      <formula>Si</formula>
      <form>Bulk</form>
      <phase>Single crystal</phase>
      <description>A variety of Si configurations including different phases, surfaces, vacancies, interstitials, dislocations and crack tips.</description>
    </material-component>
  </structure>
  <structure>
    <material-component>
      <formula>Si</formula>
      <form>Slab</form>
      <phase>Single crystal</phase>
    </material-component>
  </structure>
  <computation>
    <method-class>Electronic</method-class>
    <method>DFT</method>
    <simulation-conditions>
      <type>
        <thermodynamic-state>Equilibrium</thermodynamic-state>
      </type>
    </simulation-conditions>
    <software>
      <name>CASTEP</name>
      <role>Simulation code</role>
    </software>
  </computation>
  <dataset-location>
    <repository>Apollo - University of Cambridge Repository</repository>
    <doi>https://doi.org/10.17863/CAM.65004</doi>
    <link>https://www.repository.cam.ac.uk/handle/1810/317974</link>
  </dataset-location>
  <matcore-id>mc-ad83jsd3-1</matcore-id>
  <matcore-author>
    <name>Ellad B. Tadmor</name>
    <identifier>
      <value>https://orcid.org/0000-0003-3311-6299</value>
      <scheme>ORCID</scheme>
    </identifier>
    <affiliation>
      <name>University of Minnesota</name>
    </affiliation>
  </matcore-author>
  <matcore-date>2026-02-14</matcore-date>
  <license>
    <identifier>GPL-3.0-only</identifier>
    <name>GNU General Public License v3.0 only</name>
    <link>https://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl-3.0.en.html</link>
  </license>
</root>
```

Note: The matcore-id format in the example is tentative and subject to change.
